# Supported Mobs

> This table is generated from the entity keys in the packaged `common/src/main/resources/assets/mobchatter/dialogue.json.xz` and lists exactly **6,507 runtime entity IDs**: 90 vanilla and 6,417 modded entities. Resource packs can add or override entries, so this list reflects the repository's built-in library only, not player-installed resource-pack patches.

The name and Chinese-name columns come from the per-mob data table; when multiple mod entries point to the same entity ID, it is listed once. If a name is missing, the entity ID is used. Rows are sorted lexicographically by entity ID.

| Entity ID | English name | Chinese name | Per-mob source |

Data sources: the packaged XZ resource and `output/mob_descriptions.json`. Data rows and entity IDs differ: data is recorded per mod × mob, while runtime data is merged by global entity ID. An entity having an ID in the resource means optional dialogue data exists; it does not guarantee a line will trigger at any moment. Triggering depends on game events, distance, cooldown, and probability settings.
